parser.add_argument(\\\"--fast\\\", action=\\\"store_true\\\", help=\\\"Use fast O(1) ISR\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--timeout\\\", type=int, default=5)\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n cases\\n    python scripts/sta_prolog.py --file FILE     # evaluate a JSONL file\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport sys\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))\\n\\nfrom src.solvers.factory import get_solver\\nfrom src.sta.prolog_reward import (\\n    fast_prolog_sta_reward,\\n    parse_prolog_formalization,\\n    prolog_sta_reward,\\n)\\n\\n\\nTEST_CASES = [\\n    {\\n        \\\"name\\\": \\\"grandparent (all necessary)\\\",\\n        \\\"formalization\\\": (\\n            \\\"parent(tom, bob).\\\\n\\\"\\n            \\\"parent(bob, ann).\\\\n\\\"\\n            \\\"grandparent(X, Y) :- parent(X, Z), parent(Z, Y).\\\\n\\\"\\n            \\\"?- grandparent(tom, ann).\\\\n\\\"\\n        ),\\n        \\\"expect_proved\\\": True,\\n        \\\"expect_isr_range\\\": (0.9, 1.0),\\n        \\\"fast_isr_range\\\": (0.9, 1.0),\\n    },\\n    {\\n        \\\"name\\\": \\\"grandparent with redundant fact\\\",\\n        \\\"formalization\\\": (\\n            \\\"parent(tom, bob).\\\\n\\\"\\n            \\\"parent(bob, ann).\\\\n\\\"\\n            \\\"parent(alice, charlie).\\\\n\\\"\\n            \\\"grandparent(X, Y) :- parent(X, Z), parent(Z, Y).\\\\n\\\"\\n            \\\"?- grandparent(tom, ann).\\\\n\\\"\\n        ),\\n        \\\"expect_proved\\\": True,\\n        \\\"expect_isr_range\\\": (0.5, 0.8),\\n        \\\"fast_isr_range\\\": (0.9, 1.0),\\n    },\\n    {\\n        \\\"name\\\": \\\"missing fact (should fail)\\\",\\n        \\\"formalization\\\": (\\n            \\\"parent(tom, bob).\\\\n\\\"\\n            \\\"grandparent(X, Y) :- parent(X, Z), parent(Z, Y).\\\\n\\\"\\n            \\\"?- grandparent(tom, ann).\\\\n\\\"\\n        ),\\n        \\\"expect_proved\\\": False,\\n        \\\"expect_isr_range\\\": (0.0, 0.0),\\n        \\\"fast_isr_range\\\": (0.0, 0.0),\\n    },\\n    {\\n        \\\"name\\\": \\\"simple fact query\\\",\\n        \\\"formalization\\\": (\\n            \\\"likes(mary, food).\\\\n\\\"\\n            \\\"likes(mary, wine).\\\\n\\\"\\n            \\\"?- likes(mary, food).\\\\n\\\"\\n        ),\\n        \\\"expect_proved\\\": True,\\n        \\\"expect_isr_range\\\": (0.4, 0.6),\\n        \\\"fast_isr_range\\\": (0.9, 1.0),\\n    },\\n    {\\n        \\\"name\\\": \\\"chain reasoning\\\",\\n        \\\"formalization\\\": (\\n            \\\"mortal(X) :- human(X).\\\\n\\\"\\n            \\\"human(X) :- greek(X).\\\\n\\\"\\n            \\\"greek(socrates).\\\\n\\\"\\n            \\\"?- mortal(socrates).\\\\n\\\"\\n        ),\\n        \\\"expect_proved\\\": True,\\n        \\\"expect_isr_range\\\": (0.9, 1.0),\\n        \\\"fast_isr_range\\\": (0.9, 1.0),\\n    },\\n]\\n\\n\\ndef run_tests(solver, use_fast: bool = False):\\n    print(f\\\"Solver: {type(solver).__name__}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"Mode: {'fast (O(1))' if use_fast else 'full (O(n))'}\\\")\\n    print(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n\\n    passed = 0\\n    for tc in TEST_CASES:\\n        name = tc[\\\"name\\\"]\\n        reward_fn = fast_prolog_sta_reward if use_fast else prolog_sta_reward\\n        result = reward_fn({}, tc[\\\"formalization\\\"], solver, timeout=5)\\n\\n        proved = result[\\\"execution_reward\\\"] > 0\\n        isr = result[\\\"sta_reward\\\"]\\n        lo, hi = tc.get(\\\"fast_isr_range\\\", tc[\\\"expect_isr_range\\\"]) if use_fast else tc[\\\"expect_isr_range\\\"]\\n\\n        proved_ok = proved == tc[\\\"expect_proved\\\"]\\n        isr_ok = lo <= isr <= hi if tc[\\\"expect_proved\\\"] else isr == 0.0\\n\\n        status = \\\"PASS\\\" if (proved_ok and isr_ok) else \\\"FAIL\\\"\\n        if status == \\\"PASS\\\":\\n            passed += 1\\n\\n        print(f\\\"\\\\n[{status}] {name}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  proved={proved} (expect={tc['expect_proved']})\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  ISR={isr:.3f} (expect=[{lo:.1f}, {hi:.1f}])\\\")\\n\\n        if \\\"components\\\" in result.get(\\\"details\\\", {}):\\n            for comp in result[\\\"details\\\"][\\\"components\\\"]:\\n                mark = \\\"*\\\" if comp[\\\"is_necessary\\\"] else \\\" \\\"\\n                print(f\\\"    [{mark}] {comp['content']}\\\")\\n\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\n{'=' * 60}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"Results: {passed}/{len(TEST_CASES)} passed\\\")\\n    return passed == len(TEST_CASES)\\n\\n\\ndef run_file(solver, path: str, use_fast: bool = False):\\n    data = []\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        for line in f:\\n            line = line.strip()\\n            if line:\\n                data.append(json.loads(line))\\n\\n    reward_fn = fast_prolog_sta_reward if use_fast else \\\"proved\\\" if proved else \\\"FAILED\\\"\\n        print(f\\\"[{i}] {item.get('id', '?'):>12s}  {status}  ISR={isr:.3f}\\\")\\n\\n    n = len(data)\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\nTotal: {n}, Proved:   parser.add_argument(\\\"--test\\\", action=\\\"store_true\\\", help=\\\"Run built-in test cases\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--file\\\", type=str, help=\\\"JSONL file to evaluate\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--fast\\\", action=\\\"store_true\\\", help=\\\"Use fast O(1) ISR\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--timeout\\\", type=int, default=5)\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    solver = get_solver(\\\"prolog\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"swipl available: {solver.is_available()}\\\")\\n\\n    if args.test:\\n        ok = run_tests(solver, use_fast=args.fast)\\n        sys.exit(0 if ok else 1)\\n    elif args.file:\\n        run_file(solver, args.file, use_fast=args.fast)\\n    else:\\n        # Default: run tests\\n        ok = run_tests(solver, use_fast=args.fast)\\n error=result.error,\\n        )\\n\\n    @staticmethod\\n    def _ensure_dot(clause: str) -> str:\\n        clause = clause.strip()\\n        if clause and not clause.endswith('.'):\\n            clause += '.'\\n        return clause\\n\\n    def _build_input(self, premises: list[str], query: str) -> str:\\n        lines = [self._ensure_dot(p) for p in premises if p.strip()]\\n        q =             )\\n\\n            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\\n                elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0\\n                return SolverResult(\\n                    success=False, proved=False,\\n                    time_seconds=elapsed, raw_output=\\\"\\\",\\n                    error=\\\"subprocess timeout\\\",\\n                )\\n        finally:\\n            try:\\n pass\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.46}}\"}",

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"SWI-Prolog backend.\\n\\nCalls the `swipl` binary via subprocess. Input is written as a temp .pl file.\\nQueries are embedded as directives: :- (Query -> halt(0) ; halt(1)).\\n\\nExit codes:\\n  0 — query succeeded (proved)\\n  1 — query failed (not proved)\\n  2 — fallback halt (syntax error / directive didn't execute)\\n\\nDependencies: swipl (SWI-Prolog) in PATH.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport os\\nimport shutil\\nimport subprocess\\nimport tempfile\\nimport time\\n\\nfrom .base import FOLSolver, SolverResult\\n\\n\\nclass PrologSolver(FOLSolver):\\n\\n    def __init__(self, binary: str = \\\"swipl\\\"):\\n        self._binary = binary\\n\\n    def is_available(self) -> bool:\\n        return shutil.which(self._binary) is not None\\n\\n    def prove(self, premises: list[str], conclusion: str, timeout: int = 5) -> SolverResult:\\n        input_text = self._build_input(premises, conclusion)\\n        return self._run(input_text, timeout)\\n\\n    def check_consistency(self, formulas: list[str], timeout: int = 5) -> SolverResult:\\n        body = \\\"\\\\n\\\".join(self._ensure_dot(f) for f in formulas)\\n        body += \\\"\\\\n:- halt(0).\\\\n\\\"\\n        result = self._run(body, timeout)\\n        return SolverResult(\\n            success=result.success,\\n            proved=result.success and result.error is None,\\n            time_seconds=result.time_seconds,\\n            raw_output=result.raw_output,\\n            error=result.error,\\n        )\\n\\n    @staticmethod\\n    def _ensure_dot(clause: str) -> str:\\n        clause = clause.strip()\\n        if clause and not clause.endswith('.'):\\n            clause += '.'\\n        return clause\\n\\n    def _build_input(self, premises: list[str], query: str) -> str:\\n        lines = [self._ensure_dot(p) for p in premises if p.strip()]\\n        q = query.strip()\\n        if q.startswith(\\\"?-\\\"):\\n            q = q[2:].strip()\\n        q = q.rstrip('.').strip()\\n        lines.append(f\\\":- ({q} -> halt(0) ; halt(1)).\\\")\\n        return \\\"\\\\n\\\".join(lines) + \\\"\\\\n\\\"\\n\\n    def _run(self, input_text: str, timeout: int) -> SolverResult:\\n        tmp_fd, tmp_path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=\\\".pl\\\", prefix=\\\"prolog_\\\")\\n        try:\\n            with os.fdopen(tmp_fd, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n                f.write(input_text)\\n\\n            t0 = time.monotonic()\\n            try:\\n                proc = subprocess.run(\\n                    [self._binary, \\\"-q\\\", \\\"-f\\\", tmp_path, \\\"-g\\\", \\\"halt(2)\\\"],\\n                    capture_output=True,\\n                    text=True,\\n                    encoding=\\\"utf-8\\\",\\n                    errors=\\\"replace\\\",\\n                    timeout=timeout + 5,\\n                )\\n                elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0\\n                raw = proc.stdout + proc.stderr\\n\\n                if proc.returncode == 0:\\n                    return SolverResult(\\n                        success=True, proved=True,\\n                        time_seconds=elapsed, raw_output=raw,\\n                    )\\n                elif proc.returncode == 1:\\n                    return SolverResult(\\n                        success=True, proved=False,\\n                        time_seconds=elapsed, raw_output=raw,\\n                    )\\n                else:\\n                    return SolverResult(\\n                        success=False, proved=False,\\n                        time_seconds=elapsed, raw_output=raw,\\n                        error=f\\\"swipl exit code {proc.returncode}\\\",\\n                    )\\n\\n            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\\n                elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0\\n                return SolverResult(\\n                    success=False, proved=False,\\n                    time_seconds=elapsed, raw_output=\\\"\\\",\\n                    error=\\\"subprocess timeout\\\",\\n                )\\n        finally:\\n            try:\\n                os.unlink(tmp_path)\\n            except OSError:\\n                pass\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.46}}"
    }
  }

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Prolog STA reward: execution + ISR for Prolog formalizations.\\n\\nMirrors src/sta/reward.py but for Prolog logic type:\\n- Parses Prolog clauses + query from model output\\n- Uses PrologSolver (swipl) instead of Prover9\\n- Ablation: remove one clause at a time, check if query breaks\\n- ISR = necessary_clauses / total_clauses\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nimport re\\nimport time\\nfrom typing import Optional\\n\\nfrom ..solvers.base import FOLSolver, SolverResult\\nfrom .ablation import AblationResult\\nfrom .components import FOLComponent\\n\\n\\ndef parse_prolog_formalization(text: str) -> tuple[list[str], str]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract Prolog clauses and query from model output.\\n\\n    Supported formats:\\n\\n    1. Inline query:\\n        parent(tom, bob).\\n        grandparent(X,Y) :- parent(X,Z), parent(Z,Y).\\n        ?- grandparent(tom, ann).\\n\\n    2. Labeled sections:\\n        Facts/Rules:\\n        parent(tom, bob).\\n        Query: grandparent(tom, ann)\\n\\n    Returns (clauses, query). Query has no '?-' prefix or trailing '.'.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    lines = text.strip().splitlines()\\n    query: Optional[str] = None\\n    clauses: list[str] = []\\n\\n    for raw_line in lines:\\n        line = raw_line.strip()\\n        if not line or line.startswith('%'):\\n            continue\\n\\n        if line.startswith('?-'):\\n            query = line[2:].strip().rstrip('.')\\n            continue\\n\\n        m = re.match(r'^Query\\\\s*:\\\\s*(.+)', line, re.IGNORECASE)\\n        if m:\\n            q = m.group(1).strip()\\n            if q.startswith('?-'):\\n                q = q[2:].strip()\\n            query = q.rstrip('.')\\n            continue\\n\\n        if re.match(\\n            r'^(Facts|Rules|Clauses|Program|Premises)'\\n            r'(\\\\s*/\\\\s*(Rules|Facts|Clauses))?\\\\s*:\\\\s*$',\\n            line, re.IGNORECASE,\\n        ):\\n            continue\\n\\n        line = re.sub(r'^\\\\d+[.)]\\\\s*', '', line)\\n\\n        if line.startswith(':- ') or line.startswith(':-\\\\t'):\\n            continue\\n\\n        if line:\\n            clauses.append(line)\\n\\n    if query is None and clauses:\\n        query = clauses.pop().rstrip('.')\\n\\n    return clauses, query or \\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef prolog_sta_ablation(\\n    clauses: list[str],\\n    query: str,\\n    solver: FOLSolver,\\n    timeout: int = 5,\\n    original_result: Optional[SolverResult] = None,\\n) -> list[AblationResult]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Remove each clause one at a time, check if query breaks.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if original_result is None:\\n        original_result = solver.prove(clauses, query, timeout=timeout)\\n\\n    results: list[AblationResult] = []\\n    for i, clause in enumerate(clauses):\\n        component = FOLComponent(type=\\\"premise\\\", content=clause, index=i)\\n        ablated = clauses[:i] + clauses[i + 1:]\\n        ablated_result = solver.prove(ablated, query, timeout=timeout)\\n\\n        results.append(AblationResult(\\n            component=component,\\n            original_proved=original_result.proved,\\n            ablated_proved=ablated_result.proved,\\n            is_necessary=original_result.proved and not ablated_result.proved,\\n            solver_result=ablated_result,\\n        ))\\n\\n    return results\\n\\n\\ndef prolog_isr(ablation_results: list[AblationResult]) -> float:\\n    if not ablation_results:\\n        return 0.0\\n    return sum(1 for r in ablation_results if r.is_necessary) / len(ablation_results)\\n\\n\\ndef prolog_sta_reward(\\n    problem: dict,\\n    formalization: str,\\n    solver: FOLSolver,\\n    execution_weight: float = 0.5,\\n    sta_weight: float = 0.5,\\n    timeout: int = 5,\\n) -> dict:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Full Prolog STA reward (O(n) solver calls).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    try:\\n        clauses, query = parse_prolog_formalization(formalization)\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        return _error_result(f\\\"parse error: {e}\\\", formalization)\\n\\n    if not query:\\n        return _error_result(\\\"empty query\\\", formalization)\\n\\n    proof = solver.prove(clauses, query, timeout=timeout)\\n    exec_reward = 1.0 if proof.proved else 0.0\\n\\n    if proof.proved:\\n        abl = prolog_sta_ablation(\\n            clauses, query, solver, timeout=timeout, original_result=proof,\\n        )\\n        isr = prolog_isr(abl)\\n        details = {\\n            \\\"clauses\\\": clauses, \\\"query\\\": query,\\n            \\\"n_clauses\\\": len(abl),\\n            \\\"n_necessary\\\": sum(1 for r in abl if r.is_necessary),\\n            \\\"isr\\\": isr,\\n            \\\"components\\\": [\\n                {\\\"content\\\": r.component.content, \\\"is_necessary\\\": r.is_necessary}\\n                for r in abl\\n            ],\\n        }\\n    else:\\n        isr = 0.0\\n        details = {\\n            \\\"clauses\\\": clauses, \\\"query\\\": query,\\n            \\\"proof_failed\\\": True, \\\"solver_error\\\": proof.error,\\n        }\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"execution_reward\\\": exec_reward,\\n <details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
All checks are complete. Let me summarize the findings:

1. **Prolog data**: No separate Prolog data file needed. The training data is `data/train_500_seed42.jsonl` (500 examples, FOL format with `context` and `question` fields). The `build_prolog_dataset` function uses the same JSONL but changes the system prompt to Prolog-specific one (`PROLOG_SYSTEM_PROMPT`). The model is asked to formalize natural language into Prolog (not FOL). So the data is the same — the difference is in the prompt template. This is correct for the experimental design: same NL input, different formalization target.


2. **Training code**: The implementation fully supports Prolog through dedicated dataset builders, reward functions that switch based on logic type, and CLI arguments for both `--logic_type prolog` and `--solver prolog`.
3. **SWI-Prolog integration**: The system has SWI-Prolog 8.4.2 installed and ready to use, with basic proof verification working correctly.
4. **Test coverage and solver**: The test suite for the Prolog solver passes all 5 tests, the `PrologSolver` class is fully implemented, and the factory pattern supports instantiating it via `get_solver("prolog")`.
5. **Configuration**: The GRPO config file is already set up with the correct data path, logic type, solver selection, execution-only reward mode, and output directory pointing to the data disk as required.
6. **Dry-run and resources**: A test run of the training script completes successfully, and there's 142GB of available disk space, which is sufficient for the planned runs.

The main gaps are that the config currently disables wandb logging and only covers seed 42—I'll need to enable wandb for actual metric tracking and create additional configs for seeds 43 and 44. The vLLM and kernels warnings are non-critical. Everything else is in place to start training.
```

Prolog 数据\n- **不需要单独的 Prolog 数据文件**。训练数据仍使用 `data/train_500_seed42.jsonl`（500 条，C001 compliant）\n- `build_prolog_dataset()` 使用相同 JSONL 但切换 prompt 为 Prolog 格式（`PROLOG_SYSTEM_PROMPT`），让模型将 NL 形式化为 Prolog 而非 FOL\n- 实验设计正确：相同 NL 输入，不同形式化目标\n\n### 2. 训练代码\n- `train_grpo.py` **完全支持** Prolog：\n  - `--logic_type prolog` + `--solver prolog` CLI 参数已实现\n  - `build_prolog_dataset()` 使用 `PROLOG_SYSTEM_PROMPT` / `PROLOG_USER_TEMPLATE`\n  - `make_reward_fn()` 在 `logic_type == \"prolog\"` 时自动切换到 `prolog_sta_reward` / `fast_prolog_sta_reward`\n- Reward 代码：`src/sta/prolog_reward.py` 完整实现（parse、ablation、ISR 计算）\n- Solver 代码：`src/solvers/prolog.py` 完整实现（`PrologSolver` via swipl subprocess）\n\n### 3. SWI-Prolog 集成\n- `swipl` 位置：`/usr/bin/swipl`，版本 8.4.2 ✅\n- 简单证明测试：PASSED ✅\n- `sta_prolog.py` 测试套件：**5/5 PASSED** ✅\n\n### 4. Config\n- `configs/grpo_prolog_exec_only.yaml` 已存在，配置正确：\n  - `data_path: data/train_500_seed42.jsonl`（C001 ✅）\n  - `logic_type: prolog`, `solver: prolog`\n  - `reward_mode: execution_only`, `exec_weight: 1.0`, `sta_weight: 0.0`\n  - `output_dir: /root/autodl-tmp/outputs/grpo_prolog_exec_only`（C003 ✅）\n  - `save_total_limit=1` hardcoded in GRPOConfig（C003 ✅）\n  - `seed: 42`\n\n### 5. Dry-run\n- `python scripts/train_grpo.py --help` 正常运行 ✅\n- 非阻塞警告：vLLM 版本兼容性提示、kernels 包缺失（CPU gemm only，不影响训练）\n\n### 6. 资源\n- 磁盘：`/root/autodl-tmp/` 142GB 可用 ✅\n- 模型：`/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B` 已就位\n\n## 启动命令模板\n\n3 seeds (42/43/44)，每次占 1 张 GPU：\n\n```bash\n# Seed 42 (GPU N)\nexport CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=N\ncd /root/solver-trace-ablation && source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\nnohup python scripts/train_grpo.py \\\n  --config configs/grpo_prolog_exec_only.yaml \\\n  --seed 42 \\\n  --output_dir /root/autodl-tmp/outputs/plan006_prolog_exec_s42 \\\n  --wandb_run plan006_prolog_exec_s42 \\\n  --no_wandb \\\n  > /root/autodl-tmp/outputs/plan006_prolog_exec_s42.log 2>&1 &\n\n# Seed 43 (GPU M)\nexport CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=M\nnohup python scripts/train_grpo.py \\\n  --config configs/grpo_prolog_exec_only.yaml \\\n  --seed 43 \\\n  --output_dir /root/autodl-tmp/outputs/plan006_prolog_exec_s43 \\\n  --wandb_run plan006_prolog_exec_s43 \\\n  --no_wandb \\\n  > /root/autodl-tmp/outputs/plan006_prolog_exec_s43.log 2>&1 &\n\n# Seed 44 (GPU M2)\nexport CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=M2\nnohup python scripts/train_grpo.py \\\n  --config configs/grpo_prolog_exec_only.yaml \\\n  --seed 44 \\\n  --output_dir /root/autodl-tmp/outputs/plan006_prolog_exec_s44 \\\n  --wandb_run plan006_prolog_exec_s44 \\\n  --no_wandb \\\n  > /root/autodl-tmp/outputs/plan006_prolog_exec_s44.log 2>&1 &\n```\n\nCLI `--seed` / `--output_dir` 会覆盖 YAML 中的默认值，无需为每个 seed 创建单独 config。\n\n## 注意事项\n- 启动前替换 N/M/M2 为实际空闲 GPU 编号（C002: 必须 export，不能 inline）\n- 如需 wandb 追踪，去掉 `--no_wandb` 并确保 `--wandb_project solver-trace-ablation`\n- 预计每 seed 训练 100 steps，单卡约 2-3 小时\n"
